\displaystyle(\mathcal{I}_{G}+\mathcal{K}_{G})^{-1}\mathcal{L}\mathcal{T}_{X}=\mathcal{R}_{G}+(\mathcal{I}_{G}+\mathcal{K}_{G})^{-1}\mathcal{K}\mathcal{R}_{B}.

Let

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}=(\mathcal{I}_{G}+\mathcal{K}_{G})^{-1}\mathcal{L}\in\mathbf{M}_{X}^{G},\ \mathcal{N}=(\mathcal{I}_{G}+\mathcal{K}_{G})^{-1}\mathcal{K}\mathcal{R}_{B}\in\mathbf{M}_{B}^{G}.

Then we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}\mathcal{T}_{X}=\mathcal{R}_{G}+\mathcal{N}.

Combining (
3.1
) and (
4.56
)–(
4.59
) implies

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{N}\|_{0}

\displaystyle\leq\|(\mathcal{I}_{G}+\mathcal{K}_{G})^{-1}\|_{0}\|\mathcal{K}\|_{0}\lesssim N_{1}^{-\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{0}},

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{M}\|_{0}

\displaystyle\leq\|(\mathcal{I}_{G}+\mathcal{K}_{G})^{-1}\|_{0}\|\mathcal{L}\|_{0}\lesssim\delta_{0}^{-2},

and for
\alpha\in(0,\alpha_{1}]
,

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{N}\|_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\|(\mathcal{I}_{G}+\mathcal{K}_{G})^{-1}\|_{\alpha}\|\mathcal{K}\|_{0}+\|(\mathcal{I}_{G}+\mathcal{K}_{G})^{-1}\|_{0}\|\mathcal{K}\|_{\alpha}\right)\lesssim\delta_{0}^{28}<1,

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{M}\|_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\|(\mathcal{I}_{G}+\mathcal{K}_{G})^{-1}\|_{\alpha}\|\mathcal{L}\|_{0}+\|(\mathcal{I}_{G}+\mathcal{K}_{G})^{-1}\|_{0}\|\mathcal{L}\|_{\alpha}\right)\lesssim\delta_{0}^{-2}.

We finish the proof.
∎

We have further

Lemma 4.6
.

Let

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}^{\prime}=\mathcal{T}_{X}\mathcal{R}_{B}-\mathcal{T}_{X}\mathcal{R}_{G}\mathcal{N}\in\mathbf{M}_{B}^{X},\ \mathcal{Z}=\mathcal{I}_{X}-\mathcal{T}_{X}\mathcal{R}_{G}\mathcal{M}\in\mathbf{M}_{X}^{X}.

(4.60)

Then

\displaystyle\mathcal{Z}\mathcal{T}_{X}=\mathcal{T}^{\prime}

(4.61)

and for
\alpha\in[0,\alpha_{1}],

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}^{\prime}\|_{\alpha}\lesssim 1,\ \|\mathcal{Z}\|_{\alpha}\lesssim\delta_{0}^{-2}.

(4.62)

Moreover,
\mathcal{R}_{B}\mathcal{T}_{X}^{-1}
is a left inverse of
\mathcal{T}^{\prime}
.

Proof.

Recalling (
4.50
) and (
4.60
), we get

\displaystyle\mathcal{Z}\mathcal{T}_{X}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{T}_{X}-\mathcal{T}_{X}\mathcal{R}_{G}\mathcal{M}\mathcal{T}_{X}=\mathcal{T}_{X}(\mathcal{I}_{X}-\mathcal{R}_{G}-\mathcal{R}_{G}\mathcal{N})

\displaystyle=\mathcal{T}_{X}\mathcal{R}_{B}-\mathcal{T}_{X}\mathcal{R}_{G}\mathcal{N}=\mathcal{T}^{\prime}.

We will prove (
4.62
). For
\alpha\in[0,\alpha_{1}]
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{X}\|_{\alpha}\leq\|\varepsilon\mathcal{W}_{X}\|_{\alpha}+\|\mathcal{D}_{X}\|\lesssim 1.

From (
3.1
), we have for
\alpha\in[0,\alpha_{1}],

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}^{\prime}\|_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathcal{T}_{X}\|_{\alpha}+\|\mathcal{T}_{X}\|_{\alpha}\|\mathcal{N}\|_{0}+\|\mathcal{T}_{X}\|_{0}\|\mathcal{N}\|_{\alpha}\lesssim 1,

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{Z}\|_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathcal{T}_{X}\|_{\alpha}\|\mathcal{M}\|_{0}+\|\mathcal{T}_{X}\|_{0}\|\mathcal{M}\|_{\alpha}\lesssim\delta_{0}^{-2}.

Finally, direct computation shows

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{B}\mathcal{T}_{X}^{-1}R_{X}\mathcal{T}=\mathcal{R}_{B}\mathcal{T}_{X}^{-1}\mathcal{T}_{X}(\mathcal{R}_{B}-\mathcal{R}_{G}\mathcal{N})=\mathcal{I}_{B}-\mathcal{R}_{B}\mathcal{R}_{G}\mathcal{N}=\mathcal{I}_{B},

which implies
\mathcal{R}_{B}\mathcal{T}_{X}^{-1}R_{X}
is a left inverse of
\mathcal{T}^{\prime}
.
∎

Now
\mathcal{T}^{\prime}\in\mathbf{M}_{B}^{X}
has a left inverse with
0
-norm
O(\delta_{1}^{-\frac{31}{15}})
. We will utilize Lemma
3.5
to obtain another left inverse
\mathcal{V}
of
\mathcal{T}^{\prime}
so that for
\alpha\in[0,\alpha_{1}],

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{V}\|_{\alpha}\lesssim\zeta_{1}^{\alpha}\delta_{1}^{-\frac{31}{15}}.

The details are given by

Lemma 4.7
.

The operator
\mathcal{T}^{\prime}
defined in (
4.60
) has a left inverse
\mathcal{V}
satisfying for
\alpha\in[0,\alpha_{1}],

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{V}\|_{\alpha}\lesssim\zeta_{1}^{\alpha}\delta_{1}^{-\frac{31}{15}}.

(4.63)

Proof.

We introduce
Y=\Lambda_{\zeta_{1}}(\bm{k})
and let
\mathcal{E}=\mathcal{R}_{Y}\mathcal{T}^{\prime}\in\mathbf{M}_{B}^{Y}
.

We claim that
\mathcal{E}
has a left inverse
\mathcal{V}
satisfying

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{V}\|_{0}<2\delta_{1}^{-\frac{31}{15}}.

In fact, let
\|\bm{l}-\bm{l}^{\prime}\|\leq\frac{{\rm diam}(Y)}{2}
and
\mathcal{P}=\mathcal{T}^{\prime}-\mathcal{E}
, which implies

\displaystyle\mathcal{P}(\bm{l},\bm{l}^{\prime})=0.

Then recalling (
3.3
) and (
4.62
), we obtain for
\alpha\in[0,\alpha_{1}],

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}\|_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\lesssim({\rm diam}(Y))^{-\alpha_{1}+\alpha}\|\mathcal{P}\|_{\alpha_{1}}\leq({\rm diam}(Y))^{-\alpha_{1}+\alpha}\|\mathcal{T}^{\prime}\|_{\alpha_{1}}\lesssim\zeta_{1}^{-\alpha_{1}+\alpha}.

Thus by (
4.3
), (
4.52
) and
\alpha_{1}>2200\tau
, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}\|_{0}\|\mathcal{R}_{B}\mathcal{T}_{X}^{-1}\|_{0}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathcal{P}\|_{0}\|\mathcal{T}_{X}^{-1}\|_{0}\lesssim\zeta_{1}^{-\alpha_{1}}\delta_{1}^{-\frac{31}{15}}

\displaystyle\lesssim N_{1}^{-\alpha_{1}+1860\tau}<\frac{1}{2}.

Hence it follows from the Lemma
3.5
that
\mathcal{E}
has a left inverse
\mathcal{V}\in\mathbf{M}_{Y}^{B}
satisfying

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{V}\|_{0}\leq 2\|\mathcal{R}_{B}\mathcal{T}_{X}^{-1}\|_{0}<2\delta_{1}^{-\frac{31}{15}}.

(4.64)

We now establish
\alpha
-norm estimates. If
\|\bm{l}-\bm{l}^{\prime}\|\geq 2{\rm diam}(Y)
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{V}(\bm{l},\bm{l}^{\prime})=0.

Using (
3.4
) and (
4.64
) yields for
\alpha\in[0,\alpha_{1}]
,

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{V}\|_{\alpha}\lesssim({\rm diam}(Y))^{\alpha}\|\mathcal{V}\|_{0}\lesssim\zeta_{1}^{\alpha}\delta_{1}^{-\frac{31}{15}}.

Finally, we can obtain

\displaystyle\mathcal{V}\mathcal{P}=\mathcal{R}_{B}\mathcal{V}\mathcal{R}_{Y}(\mathcal{T}^{\prime}-\mathcal{R}_{Y}\mathcal{T}^{\prime})=0.

Since
\mathcal{T}^{\prime}=\mathcal{E}+\mathcal{P}
and
\mathcal{V}
is a left inverse of
\mathcal{E}
,
\mathcal{V}
is a left inverse of
\mathcal{T}^{\prime}
.
∎

We are ready to prove Theorem
4.4
:

Proof of Theorem
4.4
.

Since (
4.50
), (
4.61
) and Lemma
4.7
, we obtain

\displaystyle\left(\mathcal{M}-\mathcal{N}\mathcal{V}\mathcal{Z}+\mathcal{V}\mathcal{Z}\right)\mathcal{T}_{X}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{R}_{G}+\mathcal{N}+(\mathcal{R}_{B}-\mathcal{N})\mathcal{V}\mathcal{T}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{R}_{G}\oplus\mathcal{R}_{B}=\mathcal{I}_{X},

which implies

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{X}^{-1}=\mathcal{M}-\mathcal{N}\mathcal{V}\mathcal{Z}+\mathcal{V}\mathcal{Z}.

Hence

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{B}\mathcal{T}_{X}^{-1}=\mathcal{V}\mathcal{Z},\ \mathcal{R}_{G}\mathcal{T}_{X}^{-1}=\mathcal{M}-\mathcal{N}\mathcal{R}_{B}\mathcal{T}_{X}^{-1}.

Then for
\alpha\in(0,\alpha_{1}]
, we get by using (
3.1
), (
4.51
), (
4.62
) and (
4.63
) that

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{R}_{B}\mathcal{T}_{X}^{-1}\|_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\|\mathcal{V}\|_{\alpha}\|\mathcal{Z}\|_{0}+\|\mathcal{V}\|_{0}\|\mathcal{Z}\|_{\alpha}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\zeta_{1}^{\alpha}\delta_{0}^{-2}\delta_{1}^{-\frac{31}{15}},

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{R}_{G}\mathcal{T}_{X}^{-1}\|_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathcal{M}\|_{\alpha}+\|\mathcal{N}\|_{\alpha}\|\mathcal{R}_{B}\mathcal{T}_{X}^{-1}\|_{0}+\|\mathcal{N}\|_{0}\|\mathcal{R}_{B}\mathcal{T}_{X}^{-1}\|_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\lesssim\zeta_{1}^{\alpha}\delta_{0}^{-2}\delta_{1}^{-\frac{31}{15}}.

Thus for any
\alpha\in(0,\alpha_{1}]
, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{X}^{-1}\|_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathcal{R}_{B}\mathcal{T}_{X}^{-1}\|_{\alpha}+\|\mathcal{R}_{G}\mathcal{T}_{X}^{-1}\|_{\alpha}\lesssim\zeta_{1}^{\alpha}\delta_{0}^{-2}\delta_{1}^{-\frac{31}{15}}<\zeta_{1}^{\alpha}\delta_{1}^{-\frac{7}{3}},

which concludes the proof of Theorem
4.4
.
∎

Step 3

:
Estimates of
\|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}\|_{\alpha}
for general
1
-good
\Lambda
.

In this step, we will complete the verification of
\mathscr{P}_{1}
.
We recall that a finite set
\Lambda\subset\mathbb{Z}^{d}
is 1-
{\rm good}
iff

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\Lambda\cap Q_{0}\cap\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{1}\neq\emptyset\Rightarrow\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{1}\subset\Lambda,\\
\{\bm{k}\in P_{1}:\ \tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{1}\subset\Lambda\}\cap Q_{1}=\emptyset.\end{array}\right.

We will combine the estimates of
\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{1}}^{-1}
, smoothing property and rows estimate to finish this verification.

Theorem 4.8
.

If
\Lambda
is
1
-
{\rm good}
, then

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}\|_{0}

\displaystyle<\delta_{1}^{-\frac{2}{15}}\times\sup\limits_{\{\bm{k}\in P_{1}:\ \tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{1}\subset\Lambda\}}\left(\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\right),

and for
\alpha\in(0,\alpha_{1}],

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}\|_{\alpha}

\displaystyle<\zeta_{1}^{\alpha}\delta_{1}^{-\frac{14}{3}}.

Proof.

Define

\displaystyle\tilde{P}_{1}=\{\bm{k}\in P_{1}:\ \Lambda\cap\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{1}\cap Q_{0}\neq\emptyset\}.

For
\bm{l}\in\Lambda
, let

\displaystyle U_{\bm{l}}=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\Lambda_{\frac{1}{2}N_{1}}(\bm{l})\cap\Lambda,&\text{if}\ \bm{l}\notin\bigcup_{\bm{k}\in\tilde{P}_{1}}\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{1},\\
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{1},&\text{if}\ \bm{l}\in\bigcup_{\bm{k}\in\tilde{P}_{1}}\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{1}.\end{array}\right.

Denote
\mathcal{Q}_{\bm{l}}=\varepsilon\mathcal{T}_{U_{\bm{l}}}^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{U_{\bm{l}}}\mathcal{W}\mathcal{R}_{\Lambda\setminus U_{\bm{l}}}\in\mathbf{M}_{\Lambda\setminus U_{\bm{l}}}^{U_{\bm{l}}}
. By varying
\bm{l}\in\Lambda
, we are led to considering

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}(\bm{l},\bm{l}^{\prime})=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}0,&\text{for}\ \bm{l}^{\prime}\in U_{\bm{l}}\\
\mathcal{Q}_{\bm{l}}(\bm{l},\bm{l}^{\prime}),&\text{for}\ \bm{l}^{\prime}\in\Lambda\setminus U_{\bm{l}},\end{array}\right.

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}(\bm{l},\bm{l}^{\prime})=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\mathcal{T}_{U_{\bm{l}}}^{-1}(\bm{l},\bm{l}^{\prime}),&\text{for}\ \bm{l}^{\prime}\in U_{\bm{l}},\\
0,&\text{for}\ \bm{l}^{\prime}\in\Lambda\setminus U_{\bm{l}}.\end{array}\right.

Direct computation shows

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}=\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda}+\mathcal{K}.

(4.65)

We will estimate
\mathcal{K}\in\mathbf{M}_{\Lambda}^{\Lambda}
and
\mathcal{L}\in\mathbf{M}_{\Lambda}^{\Lambda}
in the following cases.

(1) Assume
\bm{l}\notin\bigcup_{\bm{k}\in\tilde{P}_{1}}\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{1}
. Then
U_{\bm{l}}
is
0
-
{\rm good}
. Similar to the proof of Lemma
4.5
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}^{\{\bm{l}\}}\|_{\alpha_{0}}\lesssim N_{1}^{-\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{0}},

and for
\alpha\in(0,\alpha_{1}],

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}^{\{\bm{l}\}}\|_{\alpha+\alpha_{0}}\lesssim\delta_{0}^{28}<1,

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{L}^{\{\bm{l}\}}\|_{\alpha+\alpha_{0}}\lesssim\delta_{0}^{-2}.

(2) Assume
\bm{l}\in\bigcup_{\bm{k}\in\tilde{P}_{1}}\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{1}
. Then there exists some
\bm{k}\in\tilde{P}_{1}
such that
\bm{l}\in\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{1}
. By (
3.1
) and (
4.49
), we get

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{Q}_{\bm{l}}\|_{\alpha_{1}}\lesssim\varepsilon\|\mathcal{T}_{U_{\bm{l}}}^{-1}\|_{\alpha_{1}}\|\mathcal{W}_{\Lambda}\|_{\alpha_{1}}\lesssim\zeta_{1}^{\alpha_{1}}\delta_{1}^{-\frac{7}{3}}.

(4.66)

Combining (
3.1
), (
3.4
) and (
4.49
), we have for
\alpha\in(0,\alpha_{1}],

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{Q}_{\bm{l}}\|_{\alpha+\alpha_{0}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\|\mathcal{T}_{U_{\bm{l}}}^{-1}\|_{\alpha+\alpha_{0}}\|\mathcal{W}_{\Lambda}\|_{0}+\|\mathcal{T}_{U_{\bm{l}}}^{-1}\|_{0}\|\mathcal{W}_{\Lambda}\|_{\alpha+\alpha_{0}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\tilde{\zeta}_{1}^{\alpha_{0}}\|\mathcal{T}_{U_{\bm{l}}}^{-1}\|_{\alpha}\|\mathcal{W}_{\Lambda}\|_{\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{0}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\zeta_{1}^{\alpha}\tilde{\zeta}_{1}^{\alpha_{0}}\delta_{1}^{-\frac{7}{3}}.

Note that if
\bm{l}^{\prime}\in\Lambda\setminus U_{\bm{l}}
, then
\|\bm{l}-\bm{l}^{\prime}\|\geq\frac{\zeta_{1}}{2}
. This implies
\mathcal{K}^{\{\bm{l}\}}(\bm{l},\bm{l}^{\prime})=0
for
\|\bm{l}-\bm{l}^{\prime}\|<\frac{\zeta_{1}}{2}
. By (
3.3
) and (
4.66
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}^{\{\bm{l}\}}\|_{\alpha_{0}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\tilde{\zeta}_{1}^{-\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{0}}\|\mathcal{K}^{\{\bm{l}\}}\|_{\alpha_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\tilde{\zeta}_{1}^{-\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{0}}\|\mathcal{Q}_{\bm{l}}\|_{\alpha_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\zeta_{1}^{\alpha_{1}}\tilde{\zeta}_{1}^{-\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{0}}\delta_{1}^{-\frac{7}{3}}.

Similarly, for
\alpha\in(0,\alpha_{1}]
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}^{\{\bm{l}\}}\|_{\alpha+\alpha_{0}}\leq\|\mathcal{Q}_{\bm{l}}\|_{\alpha+\alpha_{0}}\lesssim\zeta_{1}^{\alpha}\tilde{\zeta}_{1}^{\alpha_{0}}\delta_{1}^{-\frac{7}{3}}.

By the definition of
U_{\bm{l}}
, if
\|\bm{l}-\bm{l}^{\prime}\|>2\tilde{\zeta}_{1}
, then
\bm{l}^{\prime}\notin U_{\bm{l}}
. This implies
\mathcal{L}^{\{\bm{l}\}}(\bm{l},\bm{l}^{\prime})=0
for
\|\bm{l}-\bm{l}^{\prime}\|>2\tilde{\zeta}_{1}
. By (
3.4
), (
4.48
) and (
4.49
), we have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{L}^{\{\bm{l}\}}\|_{\alpha_{0}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\tilde{\zeta}_{1}^{\alpha_{0}}\|\mathcal{L}^{\{\bm{l}\}}\|_{0}\leq\tilde{\zeta}_{1}^{\alpha_{0}}\|\mathcal{T}_{U_{\bm{l}}}^{-1}\|_{0}

\displaystyle\leq\tilde{\zeta}_{1}^{\alpha_{0}}\delta_{0}^{-2}\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1},

and for
\alpha\in(0,\alpha_{1}],

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{L}^{\{\bm{l}\}}\|_{\alpha+\alpha_{0}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\tilde{\zeta}_{1}^{\alpha_{0}}\|\mathcal{L}^{\{\bm{l}\}}\|_{\alpha}\leq\tilde{\zeta}_{1}^{\alpha_{0}}\|\mathcal{T}_{U_{\bm{l}}}^{-1}\|_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\lesssim\zeta_{1}^{\alpha}\tilde{\zeta}_{1}^{\alpha_{0}}\delta_{1}^{-\frac{7}{3}}.

Next, using rows estimate (cf. (
3.5
)) yields for
\alpha\in(0,\alpha_{1}],

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}\|_{0}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sup_{\bm{l}\in\Lambda}\|\mathcal{K}^{\{\bm{l}\}}\|_{\alpha_{0}}\lesssim\max\left(N_{1}^{-\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{0}},\zeta_{1}^{\alpha_{1}}\tilde{\zeta}_{1}^{-\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{0}}\delta_{1}^{-\frac{7}{3}}\right),

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}\|_{\alpha}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sup_{\bm{l}\in\Lambda}\|\mathcal{K}^{\{\bm{l}\}}\|_{\alpha+\alpha_{0}}\lesssim\zeta_{1}^{\alpha}\tilde{\zeta}_{1}^{\alpha_{0}}\delta_{1}^{-\frac{7}{3}},

and

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{L}\|_{0}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{\bm{l}\in\Lambda}\|\mathcal{L}^{\{\bm{l}\}}\|_{\alpha_{0}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\tilde{\zeta}_{1}^{\alpha_{0}}\delta_{0}^{-2}\times\sup\limits_{\{\bm{k}\in P_{1}:\ \tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{1}\subset\Lambda\}}\left(\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\right),

(4.67)

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{L}\|_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{\bm{l}\in\Lambda}\|\mathcal{L}^{\{\bm{l}\}}\|_{\alpha+\alpha_{0}}\lesssim\zeta_{1}^{\alpha}\tilde{\zeta}_{1}^{\alpha_{0}}\delta_{1}^{-\frac{7}{3}}.

(4.68)

Finally, we use tame estimate and Lemma
3.5
to finish the proof. Since
N_{1}\gg 1
and
\alpha_{1}>2200\tau
, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}\|_{0}\leq\frac{1}{2}.

Recalling Lemma
3.5
, we have that
\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda}+\mathcal{K}
is invertible and

\displaystyle\|(\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda}+\mathcal{K})^{-1}\|_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\lesssim\min\{1,\|\mathcal{K}\|_{\alpha}\}\ \text{for}\ \alpha\in[0,\alpha_{1}].

(4.69)

From (
4.65
), we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}=(\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda}+\mathcal{K})^{-1}\mathcal{L}.

Recalling (
3.1
) and (
4.67
)–(
4.69
), we have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}\|_{0}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|(\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda}+\mathcal{K})^{-1}\|_{0}\|\mathcal{L}\|_{0}

\displaystyle<\delta_{1}^{-\frac{2}{15}}\times\sup\limits_{\{\bm{k}\in P_{1}:\ \tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{1}\subset\Lambda\}}\left(\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\right)

and for
\alpha\in(0,\alpha_{1}]
,

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}\|_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\|(\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda}+\mathcal{K})^{-1}\|_{\alpha}\|\mathcal{L}\|_{0}+\|(\mathcal{I}_{\Lambda}+\mathcal{K})^{-1}\|_{0}\|\mathcal{L}\|_{\alpha}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\zeta_{1}^{\alpha}\tilde{\zeta}_{1}^{2\alpha_{0}}\delta_{0}^{-2}\delta_{1}^{-\frac{7}{3}}\times\sup\limits_{\{\bm{k}\in P_{1}:\ \tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{1}\subset\Lambda\}}\left(\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle<\zeta_{1}^{\alpha}\delta_{1}^{-\frac{14}{3}}.

∎

4.3.
The proof of Theorem
4.2
: (from
\mathscr{P}_{s}
to
\mathscr{P}_{s+1}
)

We have finished the proof of
\mathscr{P}_{1}
in the Subsection
4.2
. Assume that
\mathscr{P}_{s}
holds true. In order to complete the proof of Theorem
4.2
, it suffices to establish
\mathscr{P}_{s+1}
.

We outline the main steps of the proof. First, we construct resonant blocks
\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\ (\bm{k}\in P_{s+1})
to cover
Q_{s}
. Second ,we use the Schur complement lemma (cf. Lemma
A.2
in the Appendix
A
), resolvent identity, Cramer’s rule and Hadamard’s inequality to get

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\|_{0}<\delta_{s+1}^{-\frac{1}{15}}\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1},

where
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
is
(s+1)
-th enlarged resonant block and
\theta_{s+1}
is obtained via Rouché’s theorem as one root of the Dirichlet determinant equation
\det\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}(z-\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega})=0
. Next, we use smoothing property, rows estimate and perturbation argument to get for
\alpha\in(0,\alpha_{1}]
,

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\|_{\alpha}<\zeta_{s+1}^{\alpha}\delta_{s+1}^{-\frac{1}{3}}\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}

via a
constructive
procedure. Finally, we combine the estimates of
\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}
to obtain that of
\mathcal{T}_{\Lambda}^{-1}
in a similar way for more general
(s+1)
-
{\rm good}

\Lambda
.

Recall that

\displaystyle Q_{s}^{\pm}

\displaystyle=\{\bm{k}\in P_{s}:\ \|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}\pm\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s}\},\ Q_{s}=Q_{s}^{+}\cup Q_{s}^{-},

\displaystyle\tilde{Q}_{s}^{\pm}

\displaystyle=\{\bm{k}\in P_{s}:\ \|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}\pm\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s}^{\frac{2}{3}}\},\ \tilde{Q}_{s}=\tilde{Q}_{s}^{+}\cup\tilde{Q}_{s}^{-}.

We distinguish the verification into three steps.

Step 1

:
Estimates of
\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\|_{0}
.

In this step, we will use the resolvent indentity and Rouché’s theorem to find
\theta_{s+1}=\theta_{s+1}(E)
so that

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\|_{0}

\displaystyle<\delta_{s+1}^{-\frac{1}{15}}\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}.

(4.70)

We again divide the discussion into two cases.

Case 1

: The case
(\bm{C}1)_{s}
occurs, i.e.,

\displaystyle{\rm dist}(\tilde{Q}_{s}^{-},Q_{s}^{+})>100N_{s+1}^{3}.

(4.71)

Remark 4.2
.

We can prove similar to Remark
4.1
that

\displaystyle{\rm dist}(\tilde{Q}_{s}^{-},Q_{s}^{+})={\rm dist}(\tilde{Q}_{s}^{+},Q_{s}^{-}).

Thus (
4.71
) also implies that

\displaystyle{\rm dist}(\tilde{Q}_{s}^{+},Q_{s}^{-})>100N_{s+1}^{3}.

(4.72)

By (
4.21
) and the definitions of
Q_{s}^{\pm}
and
\tilde{Q}_{s}^{\pm}
, we obtain

\displaystyle Q_{s}^{\pm}

\displaystyle=\left\{\bm{k}\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{s-1}\bm{l}_{i}:\ \|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}\pm\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s}\right\},

(4.73)

\displaystyle\tilde{Q}_{s}^{\pm}

\displaystyle=\left\{\bm{k}\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{s-1}\bm{l}_{i}:\ \|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}\pm\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s}^{\frac{2}{3}}\right\}.

Assuming (
4.71
) holds true, we define

\displaystyle P_{s+1}=Q_{s},\ \bm{l}_{s}=\bm{0}.

(4.74)

By(
4.73
), we have

\displaystyle P_{s+1}\subset\left\{\bm{k}\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{s}\bm{l}_{i}:\ \min_{\sigma=\pm 1}(\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\sigma\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}})<\delta_{s}\right\},

(4.75)

which proves (
4.6
) in the case
(\bm{C}1)_{s+1}
. Thus from (
4.72
), we obtain for
\bm{k},\bm{k}^{\prime}\in P_{s+1}
with
\bm{k}\neq\bm{k}^{\prime}
,

\displaystyle\|\bm{k}-\bm{k}^{\prime}\|\geq\min\left(100N_{s+1}^{3},\left(\frac{\gamma}{2\delta_{s}}\right)^{\frac{1}{\tau}}\right)\geq 100N_{s+1}^{3}.

(4.76)

In the following, we associate each
\bm{k}\in P_{s+1}
with blocks
\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
and
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
so that

\displaystyle\Lambda_{N_{s+1}}(\bm{k})\subset\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\subset\Lambda_{N_{s+1}+50N_{s}^{5}}(\bm{k}),

\displaystyle\Lambda_{N_{s+1}^{3}}(\bm{k})\subset\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\subset\Lambda_{N_{s+1}^{3}+50N_{s}^{5}}(\bm{k}),

and

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\cap\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s^{\prime}}\neq\emptyset\ (s^{\prime}<s+1)\Rightarrow\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s^{\prime}}\subset\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1},\\
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\cap\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s^{\prime}}\neq\emptyset\ (s^{\prime}<s+1)\Rightarrow\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s^{\prime}}\subset\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1},\\
{\rm dist}(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1},\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s+1})>10\tilde{\zeta}_{s+1}\ \text{for}\ \bm{k}\neq\bm{k}^{\prime}\in P_{s+1}.\end{array}\right.

In addition, the translation of
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}

\displaystyle\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}\subset\mathbb{Z}^{d}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{s}\bm{l}_{i}

(4.80)

is independent of
\bm{k}\in P_{s+1}
and symmetrical about the origin. The details of proof (
4.3
) and (
4.80
) can be found in the page 23 of
[
CSZ24b
]
. In summary, we have proven
(\bm{a})_{s+1}
and
(\bm{b})_{s+1}
in the case
(\bm{C}1)_{s}
.

Now we turn to the proof of
(\bm{c})_{s+1}
. First, we have that for
\bm{k}^{\prime}\in Q_{s}\ (=P_{s+1})
,

\displaystyle\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s}\subset\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s+1}.

For each
\bm{k}\in P_{s+1}
, we define

\displaystyle A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}=A_{\bm{k}}^{s}.

Then
A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\subset\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s}
and
\#A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}=\#A_{\bm{k}}^{s}\leq 2^{s}
. It remains to show that
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
is
s
-
{\rm good}
and the set
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}
is independent of
\bm{k}\in P_{s+1}
and symmetrical about the origin. The details can be found in the page 26 of
[
CSZ24b
]
as well. This finishes the proof of
(\bm{c})_{s+1}
in the case
(\bm{C}1)_{s}
.

Next, we try to prove
(\bm{d})_{s+1}
and
(\bm{f})_{s+1}
in the case of
(\bm{C}1)_{s}
. For
\bm{k}\in Q_{s}^{-}
, we consider

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z):=\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}}(z)=((v(z+\bm{n}\cdot\bm{\omega})-E)\delta_{\bm{n},\bm{n}^{\prime}}+\varepsilon\mathcal{W})_{\bm{n}\in\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}}

defined in

\displaystyle\{z\in\mathbb{C}:\ |z-\theta_{s}|<\delta_{s}^{\frac{18}{19}}\}.

(4.81)

If
\bm{k}^{\prime}\in P_{s}
and
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s}\subset(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})
, then
0\neq\|\bm{k}^{\prime}-\bm{k}\|\leq 3N_{s+1}^{3}
. Thus

\displaystyle\|\theta+\bm{k}^{\prime}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}

\displaystyle\geq\|(\bm{k}-\bm{k}^{\prime})\cdot\bm{\omega}\|_{\mathbb{T}}-\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\gamma}{(3N_{s+1}^{3})^{\tau}}-\delta_{s}\gtrsim\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{10}}.

By (
4.72
), we have
\bm{k}^{\prime}\notin\tilde{Q}_{s}^{+}
, and thus

\displaystyle\|\theta+\bm{k}^{\prime}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}>\delta_{s}^{\frac{2}{3}}.

From
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
is
s
-
{\rm good}
(cf.
(\bm{c})_{s+1}
) and (
4.19
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\|_{0}

\displaystyle<\delta_{s}^{-\frac{2}{15}}\times\sup\limits_{\{\bm{k}^{\prime}\in P_{s}:\ \tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s}\subset(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})\}}(\|\theta+\bm{k}^{\prime}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}^{\prime}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1})

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta_{s}^{-\frac{9}{10}}.

(4.82)

One may restate as

\displaystyle\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}))_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}})^{-1}\|_{0}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta_{s}^{-\frac{9}{10}}.

Notice that

\displaystyle\|z-(\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega})\|_{\mathbb{T}}

\displaystyle\leq|z-\theta_{s}|+\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}

\displaystyle<\delta_{s}^{\frac{18}{19}}+\delta_{s}<2\delta_{s}^{\frac{18}{19}}.

(4.83)

Thus by Neumann series argument, we can show

\displaystyle\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}})^{-1}\|_{0}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2\delta_{s}^{-\frac{9}{10}}.

(4.84)

Now, we can apply the Schur complement lemma to establish desired estimates. By Lemma
A.2
,
(\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))^{-1}
is controlled by the Schur complement of
((\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k})

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(z)

\displaystyle=(\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}}-\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}}

\displaystyle\ \ \times((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}})^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}}.

Our next aim is to analyze
\det\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(z)
. Since
A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}=A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}\subset\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}
and
{\rm dist}(A_{\bm{k}}^{s},\partial\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s})>\frac{1}{2}\tilde{\zeta}_{s}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\ \mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s})-\bm{k}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s})-\bm{k}}+\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s})-\bm{k}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s})-\bm{k}}+O(\tilde{\zeta}_{s}^{-\alpha_{1}}).

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}}=\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s})-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}+O(\tilde{\zeta}_{s}^{-\alpha_{1}}).

Combining (
4.84
) and
\alpha_{1}>2200\tau
, we can get

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(z)

\displaystyle=(\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}-\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s})-\bm{k}}

\displaystyle\ \ \times((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}})^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s})-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}+O(\delta_{s}^{6}).

Since
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s}
is
(s-1)
-
{\rm good}
(cf.
(\bm{c})_{s}
), by (
4.19
) and
(
4.20
)
, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s}}^{-1}\|_{0}

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{s-1}^{-\frac{32}{15}},

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s}}^{-1}\|_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leq\zeta_{s-1}^{\alpha}\delta_{s-1}^{-\frac{14}{3}}.

Equivalently,

\displaystyle\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}))_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s})-\bm{k}})^{-1}\|_{0}

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{s-1}^{-\frac{32}{15}},

\displaystyle\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}))_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s})-\bm{k}})^{-1}\|_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leq\zeta_{s-1}^{\alpha}\delta_{s-1}^{-\frac{14}{3}}.

In the set defined by (
4.81
), we claim that

\displaystyle\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s})-\bm{k}})^{-1}\|_{0}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta_{s-1}^{-\frac{32}{15}},

(4.85)

\displaystyle\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s})-\bm{k}})^{-1}\|_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\lesssim\zeta_{s-1}^{\alpha}\delta_{s-1}^{-\frac{14}{3}}.

(4.86)

Proof of the Claim.

Define

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{1}=(\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}))_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s})-\bm{k}},\ \mathcal{T}_{2}=(\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s})-\bm{k}}.

Then
\mathcal{D}_{1}=\mathcal{T}_{1}-\mathcal{T}_{2}
is diagonal so that
\|\mathcal{D}_{1}\|_{0}=\|\mathcal{D}_{1}\|_{\alpha}\lesssim\delta_{s}^{\frac{18}{19}}
by (
1.3
) and (
4.83
). Using the Neumann series expansion yields

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{2}^{-1}=(\mathcal{I}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s})-\bm{k}}-\mathcal{T}_{1}^{-1}\mathcal{D}_{1})^{-1}\mathcal{T}_{1}^{-1}=\sum_{i=0}^{\infty}(\mathcal{T}_{1}^{-1}\mathcal{D}_{1})^{i}\mathcal{T}_{1}^{-1}.

By (
3.1
) and (
4.85
), we have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{2}^{-1}\|_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathcal{T}_{1}^{-1}\|_{\alpha}\sum_{i=0}^{\infty}(i+1)K(i+1,\alpha)(\|\mathcal{T}_{1}^{-1}\|_{0}\|\mathcal{D}_{1}\|_{0})^{i}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathcal{T}_{1}^{-1}\|_{\alpha}\sum_{i=0}^{\infty}\frac{(i+1)K(i+1,\alpha)}{2^{i}}\lesssim\|\mathcal{T}_{1}^{-1}\|_{\alpha},

where
K(n,\alpha)
is defined in (
3.2
).
∎

Next, we will use the resolvent identity and the decay of
\mathcal{W}
to estimate the difference of
\mathcal{S}_{s}
and
\mathcal{S}_{s+1}
.
For the convenience, we let
X=(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s})-\bm{k}
,
Z_{1}=\Lambda_{\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s}}{4}}\cap X
,
Z_{2}=\Lambda_{\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s}}{8}}\cap X
and
Y=(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}
. Let
\bm{m}\in X
. By the resolvent identity, we have for any
\bm{n}\in Y
,

\displaystyle((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{n})-\chi_{X}(\bm{n})((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{n})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\varepsilon\sum_{\bm{l}\in X\atop\bm{l}^{\prime}\in Y\setminus X}((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{l})\mathcal{W}(\bm{l},\bm{l}^{\prime})((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}(\bm{l}^{\prime},\bm{n}).

If
\bm{m}\in Z_{2}
, since (
3.8
),
{\rm dist}(Z_{1},Y\setminus X)\geq\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s}}{8}
and
{\rm dist}(Z_{2},X\setminus Z_{1})\geq\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s}}{8}
, we can get

\displaystyle|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})(\bm{m},\bm{n})-\chi_{X}(\bm{n})((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{n})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\bm{l}\in Z_{1}\atop\bm{l}^{\prime}\in Y\setminus X}|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{l})|\cdot|\mathcal{W}(\bm{l},\bm{l}^{\prime})|\cdot|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}(\bm{l}^{\prime},\bm{n})|

\displaystyle\ \ +\sum_{\bm{l}\in X\setminus Z_{1}\atop\bm{l}^{\prime}\in Y\setminus X}|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{l})|\cdot|\mathcal{W}(\bm{l},\bm{l}^{\prime})|\cdot|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}(\bm{l}^{\prime},\bm{n})|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\tilde{\zeta}_{s}^{-\alpha_{1}}\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}\|_{0}\left(\|\mathcal{W}\|_{\alpha_{1}}\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}\|_{0}+\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}\|_{\alpha_{1}}\|\mathcal{W}\|_{0}\right).

If
\bm{m}\in X\setminus Z_{2}
, we obtain

\displaystyle|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{n})-\chi_{X}(\bm{n})((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{n})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\bm{l}\in X\atop\bm{l}^{\prime}\in Y\setminus X}|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{l})|\cdot|\mathcal{W}(\bm{l},\bm{l}^{\prime})|\cdot|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}(\bm{l}^{\prime},\bm{n})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}\|_{0}\|\mathcal{W}\|_{0}\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}\|_{0}.

For
\bm{i}\in A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}
,
\bm{n}\in Y
, since (
3.8
),
{\rm dist}(A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k},X\setminus Z_{2})\geq\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s}}{16}
and (
4.84
)–(
4.86
), we have

\displaystyle|(\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{X}((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1})(\bm{i},\bm{n})-(\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{X}((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{X})(\bm{i},\bm{n})|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{\bm{m}\in Z_{2}}|\mathcal{W}(\bm{i},\bm{m})|\cdot|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{n})-\chi_{X}(\bm{n})((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{n})|

\displaystyle\ \ +\sum_{\bm{m}\in X\setminus Z_{2}}|\mathcal{W}(\bm{i},\bm{m})|\cdot|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{n})-\chi_{X}(\bm{n})((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{n})|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{W}\|_{0}\tilde{\zeta}_{s}^{-\alpha_{1}}\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}\|_{0}\left(\|\mathcal{W}\|_{\alpha_{1}}\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}\|_{0}+\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}\|_{\alpha_{1}}\|\mathcal{W}\|_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\ \ +\|\mathcal{W}\|_{0}\|\mathcal{W}\|_{\alpha_{1}}\tilde{\zeta}_{s}^{-\alpha_{1}}\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}\|_{0}\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}\|_{0}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N_{s}^{-3\alpha_{1}}N_{s-1}^{3\alpha_{1}}\delta_{s}^{-\frac{19}{18}}\lesssim\delta_{s}^{\frac{29\alpha_{1}}{9000\tau}-\frac{19}{18}}<\delta_{s}^{4}.

It then follows that

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{X}((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}=\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{X}((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{X}+O(\delta_{s}^{4}).

As a result,

\displaystyle\ \mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{X}((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{X}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{X}((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{X}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}+O(\delta_{s}^{4})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s}(z)\mathcal{R}_{X}((\mathcal{M}_{s}(z))_{X})^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{X}\mathcal{M}_{s}(z)\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}+O(\delta_{s}^{4})

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(z)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{M}_{s}(z)_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}-\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s}(z)\mathcal{R}_{X}((\mathcal{M}_{s}(z))_{X})^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{X}\mathcal{M}_{s}(z)\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k}}+O(\delta_{s}^{4})

\displaystyle=\mathcal{S}_{s}(z)+O(\delta_{s}^{4}),

which implies (
4.15
) for the
(s+1)
-th step. Recalling (
4.14
), (
4.16
) and (
4.81
), we have

\displaystyle|\det\mathcal{S}_{s}(z)|\gtrsim\delta_{s}^{\frac{2}{75}}\|z-\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}\cdot\|z+\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}.

By Lemma
A.4
,
\#(A_{\bm{k}}^{s}-\bm{k})\leq 2^{s}
and (
4.15
), we obtain

\displaystyle\det\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(z)

\displaystyle=\det\mathcal{S}_{s}(z)+O((2^{s})^{2}(4|v|_{R})^{2^{s}}\delta_{s}^{4})

\displaystyle=\det\mathcal{S}_{s}(z)+O(\delta_{s}^{\frac{7}{2}}).

Notice that

\displaystyle\|z+\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}

\displaystyle\geq\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}-\|z-\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}-\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}

\displaystyle>\delta_{s}^{\frac{2}{3}}-\delta_{s}^{\frac{18}{19}}-\delta_{s}

\displaystyle>\frac{1}{2}\delta_{s}^{\frac{2}{3}}.

Then we have

\displaystyle|\det\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(z)|\gtrsim\delta_{s+1}^{\frac{2}{75}}(z-\theta_{s}+r_{s+1}(z)),

where
r_{s+1}(z)
is an analytic function defined in the set of (
4.81
) with
|r_{s+1}(z)|\lesssim\delta_{s}^{\frac{7}{2}-\frac{2}{3}-\frac{2}{75}}\ll\delta_{s}^{\frac{3}{2}}\ll\delta_{s}^{\frac{18}{19}}
. Finally, by the Rouché’s theorem, the equation

\displaystyle(z-\theta_{s})+r_{s+1}(z)=0

has a unique root
\theta_{s+1}
in the set of (
4.81
) satisfying

\displaystyle|\theta_{s+1}-\theta_{s}|=|r_{s+1}(\theta_{s+1})|\ll\delta_{s}^{\frac{3}{2}},\ |(z-\theta_{s})+r_{s+1}(z)|\sim|z-\theta_{s+1}|.

(4.87)

Moreover,
\theta_{s+1}
is the unique root of
\det\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)=0
in the set of (
4.81
). Since
\|z+\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}>\frac{1}{2}\delta_{s}^{\frac{2}{3}}
and
|\theta_{s+1}-\theta_{s}|\ll\delta_{s}^{\frac{3}{2}}
, we have

\displaystyle\|z+\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}\sim\|z+\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}.

Thus, if
z
belongs to the set of (
4.81
), we get

\displaystyle|\det\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(z)|\gtrsim\delta_{s+1}^{\frac{2}{75}}\|z-\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}\cdot\|z+\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}.

(4.88)

Since
\delta_{s+1}\sim\delta_{s}^{30}
, we obtain
\delta_{s+1}^{\frac{1}{2}}\ll\frac{1}{2}\delta_{s}^{\frac{18}{19}}
. Recalling (
4.81
) and (
4.87
), we see that (
4.88
) remains valid for
z
satisfying

\displaystyle\|z-\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s+1}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Now, for
\bm{k}\in Q_{s}^{+}
, we consider
\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)
in the set

\displaystyle\{z\in\mathbb{C}:\ |z+\theta_{s}|<\delta_{s}^{\frac{18}{19}}\}.

(4.89)

The similar argument shows that
\det\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)=0
has a unique root
\theta_{s+1}^{{}^{\prime}}
in the set of (
4.89
). We will show
\theta_{s+1}+\theta_{s+1}^{{}^{\prime}}=0
. In fact, by Lemma
A.3
,
\det\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)
is an even function of
z
. Then the uniqueness of the root implies
\theta_{s+1}^{{}^{\prime}}=-\theta_{s+1}
. Thus for
z
being in the set of (
4.89
), we also have (
4.88
). In conclusion, (
4.88
) is established for
z
being in

\displaystyle\{z\in\mathbb{C}:\min_{\sigma=\pm 1}\|z+\sigma\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s+1}^{\frac{1}{2}}\},

which proves (
4.16
) for the
(s+1)
-th step. Combining (
4.73
)–(
4.74
) and the following

\displaystyle\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}\pm\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<10\delta_{s+1}^{\frac{2}{3}},\ |\theta_{s+1}-\theta_{s}|<\delta_{s}^{\frac{3}{2}}\Rightarrow\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}\pm\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s},

we get

\displaystyle\left\{\bm{k}\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{s}\bm{l}_{i}:\ \min_{\sigma=\pm 1}\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\sigma\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<10\delta_{s+1}^{\frac{2}{3}}\right\}\subset P_{s+1},

which proves (
4.21
) at the
(s+1)
-th step. Finally, we want to estimate
\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}
. For
\bm{k}\in P_{s+1}
, by (
4.75
), we obtain

\displaystyle\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}\in\{z\in\mathbb{C}:\ \min_{\sigma=\pm 1}\|z+\sigma\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s}^{\frac{18}{19}}\},

which together with (
4.88
) implies

\displaystyle\ |\det(\mathcal{T}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}-\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}\mathcal{R}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}})|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ |\det\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega})|

\displaystyle\gtrsim

\displaystyle\ \delta_{s+1}^{\frac{2}{75}}\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}.

By (
4.15
), Cramer’s rule and Lemma
3.6
, one has

\displaystyle\|(\mathcal{T}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}-\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}\mathcal{R}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}})^{-1}\|_{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|\det\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega})|^{-1}\|(\mathcal{T}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}-\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}\mathcal{R}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}})^{*}\|_{0}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(2^{s})^{2}(4|v|_{R})^{2^{s}}\delta_{s+1}^{-\frac{2}{75}}\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}.

From Lemma
A.2
and (
4.82
), we get

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\|_{0}

\displaystyle<4(1+\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\|_{0})^{2}

\displaystyle\times(1+\|(\mathcal{T}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}-\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}\mathcal{R}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}})^{-1}\|_{0})

\displaystyle<\delta_{s+1}^{-\frac{1}{15}}\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s+1}\|^{-1}_{\mathbb{T}}.

(4.90)

Case 2.

The case
(\bm{C}2)_{s}
occurs, i.e.,

\displaystyle{\rm dist}(\tilde{Q}_{s}^{-},Q_{s}^{+})\leq 100N_{s+1}^{3}.

Then there exist
\bm{i}_{s}\in Q_{s}^{+}
and
\bm{j}_{s}\in\tilde{Q}_{s}^{-}
with
\|\bm{i}_{s}-\bm{j}_{s}\|\leq 100N_{s+1}^{3}
such that

\displaystyle\|\theta+\bm{i}_{s}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s},\ \|\theta+\bm{j}_{s}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s}^{\frac{2}{3}}.

Define

\displaystyle\bm{l}_{s}=\bm{i}_{s}-\bm{j}_{s}.

Using (
4.6
) and (
4
) yields

\displaystyle Q_{s}^{+},\tilde{Q}_{s}^{-}\subset P_{s}\subset\mathbb{Z}^{d}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{s-1}\bm{l}_{i}.

Thus
\bm{i}_{s}\equiv\bm{j}_{s}\ (\text{mod}\ \mathbb{Z}^{d})
and
\bm{l}_{s}\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}
. Define

\displaystyle O_{s+1}=Q_{s}^{-}\cup(Q_{s}^{+}-\bm{l}_{s}).

(4.91)

For every
\bm{o}\in O_{s+1}
, define its mirror point as

\displaystyle\bm{o}^{*}=\bm{o}+\bm{l}_{s}.

Then we have

\displaystyle O_{s+1}\subset\{\bm{o}\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{s-1}\bm{l}_{i}:\ \|\theta+\bm{o}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<2\delta_{s}^{\frac{2}{3}}\}

and

\displaystyle O_{s+1}+\bm{l}_{s}\subset\{\bm{o}^{*}\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{s-1}\bm{l}_{i}:\ \|\theta+\bm{o}^{*}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<2\delta_{s}^{\frac{2}{3}}\}.

Then by (
4.21
), we obtain

\displaystyle O_{s+1}\cup(O_{s+1}+\bm{l}_{s})\subset P_{s}.

(4.92)

Next, define

\displaystyle P_{s+1}=\left\{\frac{1}{2}(\bm{o}+\bm{o}^{*}):\ \bm{o}\in O_{s+1}\right\}=\left\{\bm{o}+\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}:\ \bm{o}\in O_{s+1}\right\}.

(4.93)

Notice that

\displaystyle\min

\displaystyle\left(\left\|\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}},\left\|\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}-\frac{1}{2}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\|\bm{l}_{s}\cdot\bm{\omega}+2\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}(\|\theta+\bm{i}_{s}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}+\|\theta+\bm{j}_{s}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}})<\delta_{s}^{\frac{2}{3}}.

Since
\delta_{s}\ll 1
, only one of

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{0}^{\frac{2}{3}}\ \text{and}\ \left\|\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}-\frac{1}{2}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{0}^{\frac{2}{3}}

occurs. First, we consider the case of

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s}^{\frac{2}{3}}.

(4.94)

Let
\bm{k}\in P_{s+1}
. Since
\bm{k}=\bm{o}+\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}
(for some
\bm{o}\in O_{s+1}
), we have

\displaystyle\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}\|_{\mathbb{T}}\leq\|\theta+\bm{o}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}+\left\|\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}<3\delta_{s}^{\frac{2}{3}},

(4.95)

which implies

\displaystyle P_{s+1}\subset\{\bm{k}\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{s}\bm{l}_{i}:\ \|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<3\delta_{s}^{\frac{2}{3}}\}.

(4.96)

Moreover, if
\bm{k}\neq\bm{k}^{\prime}\in P_{s+1}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\bm{k}-\bm{k}^{\prime}\|\geq\left(\frac{\gamma}{6\delta_{s}^{\frac{2}{3}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{\tau}}\sim N_{s+1}^{20}\gg 100N_{s+1}^{5}.

Similar to the proof that appears in
Case 1
(i.e., the
(\bm{C}1)_{s}
case), we can associate each
\bm{k}\in P_{s+1}
with the blocks
\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
and
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
satisfying

\displaystyle\Lambda_{100N_{s+1}^{3}}(\bm{k})\subset\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\subset\Lambda_{100N_{s+1}^{3}+50N_{s}^{5}}(\bm{k}),

\displaystyle\Lambda_{N_{s+1}^{5}}(\bm{k})\subset\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\subset\Lambda_{N_{s+1}^{5}+50N_{s}^{5}}(\bm{k})

and

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\cap\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s^{\prime}}\neq\emptyset\ (s^{\prime}<s+1)\Rightarrow\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s^{\prime}}\subset\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1},\\
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\cap\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s^{\prime}}\neq\emptyset\ (s^{\prime}<s+1)\Rightarrow\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s^{\prime}}\subset\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1},\\
{\rm dist}(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1},\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s+1})>10\tilde{\zeta}_{s+1}\ \text{for}\ \bm{k}\neq\bm{k}^{\prime}\in P_{s+1}.\end{array}\right.

In addition, the translation

\displaystyle\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}\subset\mathbb{Z}^{d}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{s}\bm{l}_{i}

is independent of
\bm{k}\in P_{s+1}
and symmetrical about the origin. In summary, we have proven
(\bm{a})_{s+1}
and
(\bm{b})_{s+1}
in the case of
(\bm{C}2)_{s}
.

For each
\bm{k}\in P_{s+1}
, we have
\bm{o},\bm{o}^{*}\in P_{s}
by (
4.92
). Define

\displaystyle A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}=A_{\bm{o}}^{s}\cup A_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s},

where
\bm{o}\in O_{s+1}
and
\bm{k}=\frac{\bm{o}+\bm{o}^{*}}{2}
(see (
4.93
)). Then

\displaystyle A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}

\displaystyle\subset\Omega_{\bm{o}}^{s}\cup\Omega_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s}\subset\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1},

\displaystyle\#A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}

\displaystyle=\#A_{\bm{o}}^{s}+\#A_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s}\leq 2^{s+1}.

The proof of
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
is
s
-
{\rm good}
can be found at page 32 of
[
CSZ24b
]
. This finishes the proof of
(\bm{c})_{s+1}
in the case of
(\bm{C}2)_{s}
.

Consider

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z):=\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}}(z)=((v(z+\bm{n}\cdot\bm{\omega})-E)\delta_{\bm{n},\bm{n}^{\prime}}+\varepsilon\mathcal{W})_{\bm{n}\in\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}}

in the set

\displaystyle\{z\in\mathbb{C}:\ |z|<\delta_{s}^{\frac{5}{8}}\}.

(4.100)

If
\bm{k}^{\prime}\in P_{s}
and
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s}\subset(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})
, then
\bm{k}^{\prime}\neq\bm{o},\bm{o}^{*}
and
\|\bm{k}^{\prime}-\bm{o}\|,\|\bm{k}^{\prime}-\bm{o}^{*}\|\leq 4N_{s+1}^{5}
. Thus

\displaystyle\|\theta+\bm{k}^{\prime}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}

\displaystyle\geq\|(\bm{k}^{\prime}-\bm{o})\cdot\bm{\omega}\|_{\mathbb{T}}-\|\theta+\bm{o}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\gamma}{(4N_{s+1}^{5})^{\tau}}-2\delta_{s}^{\frac{2}{3}}\gtrsim\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{6}}

and

\displaystyle\|\theta+\bm{k}^{\prime}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}

\displaystyle\geq\|(\bm{k}^{\prime}-\bm{o}^{*})\cdot\bm{\omega}\|_{\mathbb{T}}-\|\theta+\bm{o}^{*}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{\gamma}{(4N_{s+1}^{5})^{\tau}}-2\delta_{s}^{\frac{2}{3}}\gtrsim\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{6}}.

By (
4.19
) and
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
is
s
-
{\rm good}
(cf.
(\bm{c})_{s+1}
), we have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\|_{0}

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{s}^{-\frac{2}{15}}\times\sup\limits_{\{\bm{k}^{\prime}\in P_{s}:\ \tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}^{\prime}}^{s}\subset(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})\}}(\|\theta+\bm{k}^{\prime}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}^{\prime}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1})

\displaystyle<\delta_{s}^{-\frac{7}{15}}.

(4.101)

One may restate (
4.101
) as

\displaystyle\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}))_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}})^{-1}\|_{0}<\delta_{s}^{-\frac{7}{15}}.

Since

\displaystyle\|z-(\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega})\|_{\mathbb{T}}

\displaystyle\leq|z|+\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}\|_{\mathbb{T}}

\displaystyle<\delta_{s}^{\frac{5}{8}}+3\delta_{s}^{\frac{2}{3}}<2\delta_{s}^{\frac{5}{8}},

(4.102)

using Neumann series argument shows

\displaystyle\|(\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}})^{-1}\|_{0}\lesssim\delta_{s}^{-\frac{7}{15}}.

(4.103)

Thus by Lemma
A.2
,
(\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))^{-1}
is controlled by the Schur complement of
((\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k})
, i.e.,

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(z)

\displaystyle=(\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}}-\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}}

\displaystyle\ \ \times((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}})^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}}.

Our next aim is to analyze
\det\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(z)
. Since

\displaystyle A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}

\displaystyle=(A_{\bm{o}}^{s}-\bm{k})\cup(A_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s}-\bm{k}),

\displaystyle A_{\bm{o}}^{s}-\bm{k}

\displaystyle\subset\Omega_{\bm{o}}^{s}-\bm{k},\ A_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s}-\bm{k}\subset\Omega_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s}-\bm{k}

and

\displaystyle{\rm dist}(\Omega_{\bm{o}}^{s}-\bm{k},\Omega_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s}-\bm{k})>10\tilde{\zeta}_{s},

we have

\displaystyle(\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k}}=((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{A_{\bm{o}}^{s}-\bm{k}})\oplus((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{A_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s}-\bm{k}}).

From
{\rm dist}(A_{\bm{o}}^{s},\partial\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{o}}^{s})>\frac{1}{2}\tilde{\zeta}_{s}
and
{\rm dist}(A_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s},\partial\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s})>\frac{1}{2}\tilde{\zeta}_{s}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{o}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{o}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{o}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{o}}^{s})-\bm{k}}+O(\delta_{s}^{6}),

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{o}}^{s})-\bm{k}}+O(\delta_{s}^{6}).

For the convenience, we define

\displaystyle X=(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{o}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{o}}^{s})-\bm{k},\ X^{*}=(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s})-k,\ Y=(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k},

\displaystyle Z_{1}=\Lambda_{\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s}}{4}}\cap X,\ Z_{2}=\Lambda_{\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s}}{8}}\cap X,\ Z_{1}^{*}=\Lambda_{\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s}}{4}}\cap X^{*},\ Z_{2}^{*}=\Lambda_{\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s}}{8}}\cap X^{*}.

Then direct computation yields

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(z)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{A_{\bm{o}}^{s}-\bm{k}})\oplus((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{A_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s}-\bm{k}})-(\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{o}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\oplus\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{o}*}^{s}-\bm{k}})\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}}

\displaystyle\ \ \times((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}})^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{(\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1})-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)(\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{o}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\oplus\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{o}*}^{s}-\bm{k}}).

Since
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{o}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{o}}^{s}
is
(s-1)
-
{\rm good}
, by (
4.19
) and
(
4.20
)
, we get

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{o}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{o}}^{s}}^{-1}\|_{0}

\displaystyle<\delta_{s-1}^{-\frac{32}{15}},

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{o}}^{s}\setminus A_{\bm{o}}^{s}}^{-1}\|_{\alpha}

\displaystyle<\zeta_{s-1}^{\alpha}\delta_{s-1}^{-\frac{14}{3}}.

Equivalently,

\displaystyle\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}))_{X})^{-1}\|_{0}

\displaystyle<\delta_{s-1}^{-\frac{32}{15}},

\displaystyle\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}))_{X})^{-1}\|_{\alpha}

\displaystyle<\zeta_{s-1}^{\alpha}\delta_{s-1}^{-\frac{14}{3}}.

From (
4.102
), we deduce by the same argument as (
4.85
) and (
4.86
) that

\displaystyle\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}\|_{0}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta_{s-1}^{-\frac{32}{15}},

(4.104)

\displaystyle\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}\|_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\lesssim\zeta_{s-1}^{\alpha}\delta_{s-1}^{-\frac{14}{3}}.

(4.105)

Let
\bm{m}\in X
. By the resolvent identity, we have for any
\bm{n}\in Y
,

\displaystyle((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{n})-\chi_{X}(\bm{n})((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{n})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\varepsilon\sum_{\bm{l}\in X\atop\bm{l}^{\prime}\in Y\setminus X}((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{l})\mathcal{W}(\bm{l},\bm{l}^{\prime})((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}(\bm{l}^{\prime},\bm{n}).

If
\bm{m}\in Z_{2}
, since (
3.8
),
{\rm dist}(Z_{1},Y\setminus X)\geq\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s}}{8}
and
{\rm dist}(Z_{2},X\setminus Z_{1})\geq\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s}}{8}
, we get

\displaystyle|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})(\bm{m},\bm{n})-\chi_{X}(\bm{n})((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{n})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\bm{l}\in Z_{1}\atop\bm{l}^{\prime}\in Y\setminus X}|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{l})|\cdot|\mathcal{W}(\bm{l},\bm{l}^{\prime})|\cdot|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}(\bm{l}^{\prime},\bm{n})|

\displaystyle\ \ +\sum_{\bm{l}\in X\setminus Z_{1}\atop\bm{l}^{\prime}\in Y\setminus X}|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{l})|\cdot|\mathcal{W}(\bm{l},\bm{l}^{\prime})|\cdot|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}(\bm{l}^{\prime},\bm{n})|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\tilde{\zeta}_{s}^{-\alpha_{1}}\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}\|_{0}\left(\|\mathcal{W}\|_{\alpha_{1}}\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}\|_{0}+\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}\|_{\alpha_{1}}\|\mathcal{W}\|_{0}\right).

If
\bm{m}\in X\setminus Z_{2}
, we obtain

\displaystyle|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{n})-\chi_{X}(\bm{n})((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{n})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\bm{l}\in X\atop\bm{l}^{\prime}\in Y\setminus X}|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{l})|\cdot|\mathcal{W}(\bm{l},\bm{l}^{\prime})|\cdot|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}(\bm{l}^{\prime},\bm{n})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}\|_{0}\|\mathcal{W}\|_{0}\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}\|_{0}.

For
\bm{i}\in A_{\bm{o}}^{s}-\bm{k}
,
\bm{n}\in Y
, since (
3.8
),
{\rm dist}(A_{\bm{o}}^{s}-\bm{k},X\setminus Z_{2})\geq\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{s}}{16}
and (
4.103
)–(
4.105
), we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{o}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{X}((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}(\bm{i},\bm{n})-\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{o}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{X}((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{X}(\bm{i},\bm{n})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\bm{m}\in Z_{2}}|\mathcal{W}(\bm{i},\bm{m})|\cdot|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{n})-\chi_{X}(\bm{n})((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{n})|

\displaystyle\ \ +\sum_{\bm{m}\in X\setminus Z_{2}}|\mathcal{W}(\bm{i},\bm{m})|\cdot|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{n})-\chi_{X}(\bm{n})((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}(\bm{m},\bm{n})|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{W}\|_{0}\tilde{\zeta}_{s}^{-\alpha_{1}}\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}\|_{0}\left(\|\mathcal{W}\|_{\alpha_{1}}\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}\|_{0}+\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}\|_{\alpha_{1}}\|\mathcal{W}\|_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\ \ +\|\mathcal{W}\|_{0}\|\mathcal{W}\|_{\alpha_{1}}\tilde{\zeta}_{s}^{-\alpha_{1}}\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}\|_{0}\|((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}\|_{0}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle N_{s}^{-3\alpha_{1}}N_{s-1}^{3\alpha_{1}}\delta_{s}^{-\frac{28}{45}}\lesssim\delta_{s}^{\frac{29\alpha_{1}}{9000\tau}-\frac{28}{45}}<\delta_{s}^{4}.

It then follows that

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{o}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{X}((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}=\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{o}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{X}((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{X}+O(\delta_{s}^{4}).

Similarly,

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{X^{*}}((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{Y})^{-1}=\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{X^{*}}((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X^{*}})^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{X^{*}}+O(\delta_{s}^{4}).

As a result,

\displaystyle\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(z)

\displaystyle=\left((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{A_{\bm{o}}^{s}-\bm{k}}-\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{o}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{X}((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X})^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{X}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{o}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\right)

\displaystyle\ \ \oplus\left((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{A_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s}-\bm{k}}-\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{X^{*}}((\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z))_{X^{*}})^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{X^{*}}\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{o}^{*}}^{s}-\bm{k}}\right)

\displaystyle\ \ +O(\delta_{s}^{4})

\displaystyle=\mathcal{S}_{s}\left(z-\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}\right)\oplus\mathcal{S}_{s}\left(z+\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}\right)+O(\delta_{s}^{4}).

From (
4.94
) and (
4.100
), we have

\displaystyle\left\|z-\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}\leq|z|+\left\|\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s}^{\frac{5}{8}}+\delta_{s}^{\frac{2}{3}}<2\delta_{s}^{\frac{5}{8}}

and

\displaystyle\left\|z+\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}|\leq|z|+\left\|\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s}^{\frac{5}{8}}+\delta_{s}^{\frac{2}{3}}<2\delta_{s}^{\frac{5}{8}}.

Thus, both
z-\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}
and
z+\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}
belong to the set of (
4.14
), which together with (
4.16
) implies

\displaystyle\left|\det\mathcal{S}_{s}\left(z-\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\gtrsim\delta_{s}^{\frac{2}{75}}\left\|\left(z-\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}\right)-\theta_{s}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}\cdot\left\|\left(z-\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}\right)+\theta_{s}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}},

(4.106)

\displaystyle\left|\det\mathcal{S}_{s}\left(z+\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\gtrsim\delta_{s}^{\frac{2}{75}}\left\|\left(z+\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}\right)-\theta_{s}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}\cdot\left\|\left(z+\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}\right)+\theta_{s}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}.

(4.107)

Moreover, since
\#(A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}-\bm{k})\leq 2^{s+1}
, (
4.15
) and Lemma
A.4
, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(z)\|_{0}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathcal{S}_{s}\left(z-\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}\right)\oplus\mathcal{S}_{s}\left(z+\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}\right)\right\|_{0}+O(\delta_{s}^{3})

\displaystyle\leq 2|v|_{R}+\sum_{i=0}^{s}\delta_{s}<4|v|_{R},

(4.108)

and

\displaystyle\det\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(z)

\displaystyle=\det\mathcal{S}_{s}\left(z-\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}\right)\cdot\det\mathcal{S}_{s}\left(z+\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}\right)+O((2^{s+1})^{2}(4|v|_{R})^{2^{s+1}}\delta_{s}^{4})

\displaystyle=\det\mathcal{S}_{s}\left(z-\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}\right)\cdot\det\mathcal{S}_{s}\left(z+\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}\right)+O(\delta_{s}^{\frac{7}{2}}).

(4.109)

Notice that

\displaystyle\left\|z+\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}

\displaystyle\geq\|\bm{l}_{s}\cdot\bm{\omega}\|_{\mathbb{T}}-\left\|z-\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{0}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}

\displaystyle>\frac{\gamma}{(100N_{s+1}^{3})^{\tau}}-2\delta_{s}^{\frac{5}{8}}\gtrsim\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{10}},

(4.110)

and

\displaystyle\left\|z-\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}

\displaystyle\geq\|\bm{l}_{s}\cdot\bm{\omega}\|_{\mathbb{T}}-\left\|z+\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{0}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}

\displaystyle>\frac{\gamma}{(100N_{s+1}^{3})^{\tau}}-2\delta_{s}^{\frac{5}{8}}\gtrsim\delta_{s}^{\frac{1}{10}}.

(4.111)

Let
z_{s+1}
satisfy

\displaystyle z_{s+1}\equiv\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\ (\text{mod}\ \mathbb{Z}),\ |z_{s+1}|=\left\|\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s}^{\frac{2}{3}}.

(4.112)

From (
4.106
)–(
4.111
), we get

\displaystyle|\det\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(z)|\gtrsim\delta_{s+1}^{\frac{2}{75}}((z-z_{s+1})\cdot(z+z_{s+1})+r_{s+1}(z)),

where
r_{s+1}(z)
is an analytic function in the set of (
4.100
) with

\displaystyle|r_{s+1}(z)|\lesssim\delta_{s}^{\frac{7}{2}-\frac{4}{75}-\frac{1}{5}}\ll\delta_{s}^{3}\ll\delta_{s}^{\frac{5}{8}}.

(4.113)

By the Rouché’s theorem, the equation

\displaystyle(z-z_{s+1})\cdot(z+z_{s+1})+r_{s+1}(z)=0

has exactly two roots
\theta_{s+1}
and
\theta_{s+1}^{{}^{\prime}}
in the set of (
4.100
), which are perturbations of
\pm z_{s+1}
. Notice that

\displaystyle\{|z|<\delta_{s}^{\frac{5}{8}}:\ \det\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)=0\}=\{|z|<\delta_{s}^{\frac{5}{8}}:\ \det\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(z)=0\}

and
\det\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)
is an even function of
z
. Thus

\displaystyle\theta_{s+1}^{{}^{\prime}}=-\theta_{s+1}.

If

\displaystyle|z_{s+1}-\theta_{s+1}|>|r_{s+1}(\theta_{s+1})|^{\frac{1}{2}}\ \text{and}\ |z_{s+1}+\theta_{s+1}|>|r_{s+1}(\theta_{s+1})|^{\frac{1}{2}},

then

\displaystyle|r_{s+1}(\theta_{s+1})|>|z_{s+1}-\theta_{s+1}|\cdot|z_{s+1}+\theta_{s+1}|>|r_{s+1}(\theta_{s+1})|,

which is a contradiction. Without loss of generality, we assume that

\displaystyle|\theta_{s+1}-z_{s+1}|\leq|r_{s+1}(\theta_{s+1})|^{\frac{1}{2}}<\delta_{s}^{\frac{3}{2}}.

(4.114)

Moreover, since (
4.112
) and (
4.113
), we get for
|z|=\delta_{s}^{\frac{5}{8}}
,

\displaystyle\frac{|r_{s+1}(z)-r_{s+1}(\theta_{s+1})|}{|(z-z_{s+1})\cdot(z+z_{s+1})+r_{s+1}(\theta_{s+1})|}\leq 2\delta_{s}^{\frac{7}{4}},

which combined with
\theta_{s+1}^{2}-z_{s+1}^{2}+r_{s+1}(\theta_{s+1})=0
shows

\displaystyle\ \frac{|(z-z_{s+1})\cdot(z+z_{s+1})+r_{s+1}(z)|}{|(z-\theta_{s+1})\cdot(z+\theta_{s+1})|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \frac{|(z-z_{s+1})\cdot(z+z_{s+1})+r_{s+1}(z)|}{|(z-z_{s+1})\cdot(z+z_{s+1})+r_{s+1}(\theta_{s+1})|}

\displaystyle\in

\displaystyle\ \left[1-2\delta_{s}^{\frac{7}{4}},1+2\delta_{s}^{\frac{7}{4}}\right].

By the maximum modulus principle, we have

\displaystyle|(z-z_{s+1})(z+z_{s+1})+r_{s+1}(z)|\sim|(z-\theta_{s+1})(z+\theta_{s+1})|.

Thus for
z
being in the set of (
4.100
), we have

\displaystyle|\det\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(z)|\gtrsim\delta_{s+1}^{\frac{2}{75}}\|z-\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}\cdot\|z+\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}.

(4.115)

Given
\delta_{s+1}^{\frac{1}{2}}<\frac{1}{2}\delta_{s}^{\frac{5}{8}}
, the combination of (
4.112
) and (
4.114
) implies

\displaystyle\{z\in\mathbb{C}\ :\ \min_{\sigma=\pm 1}|z+\sigma\theta_{s+1}|<\delta_{s+1}^{\frac{1}{2}}\}\subset\{z\in\mathbb{C}\ :\ |z|<\delta_{s}^{\frac{5}{8}}\}.

Hence, (
4.115
) also holds true for
z
being in

\displaystyle\{z\in\mathbb{C}:\ \|z\pm\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s+1}^{\frac{1}{2}}\},

which proves (
4.16
) for the
(s+1)
-th step. Notice that

\displaystyle\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<10\delta_{s+1}^{\frac{2}{3}},\ |\theta_{s+1}-z_{s+1}|<\delta_{s}^{\frac{3}{2}}\Rightarrow\left\|\theta+\left(\bm{k}+\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\right)\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s}.

Thus if

\displaystyle\bm{k}\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{s}\bm{l}_{i}\ \text{and}\ \|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<10\delta_{s+1}^{\frac{2}{3}},

then

\displaystyle\bm{k}+\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{s-1}\bm{l}_{i}\ \text{and}\ \left\|\theta+\left(\bm{k}+\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\right)\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s}.

Therefore, by (
4.73
), we have
\bm{k}+\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\in Q_{s}^{+}
. Recalling (
4.91
) and (
4.93
), we have
\bm{k}\in P_{s+1}
. Thus

\displaystyle\left\{\bm{k}\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{s}\bm{l}_{i}:\ \|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<10\delta_{s+1}^{\frac{2}{3}}\right\}\subset P_{s+1}.

Similarly,

\displaystyle\left\{\bm{k}\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{s}\bm{l}_{i}:\ \|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<10\delta_{s+1}^{\frac{2}{3}}\right\}\subset P_{s+1}.

Hence, we prove
(
4.21
)
for the
(s+1)
-th step.

Finally, we estimate
\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}
. For
\bm{k}\in P_{s+1}
, by (
4.95
), we have

\displaystyle\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}\in\{z\in\mathbb{C}:\ \|z\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s}^{\frac{5}{8}}\}.

Thus from (
4.115
), we obtain

\displaystyle|\det(\mathcal{T}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}-\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}\mathcal{R}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}})|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|\det\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega})|

\displaystyle\gtrsim

\displaystyle\delta_{s+1}^{\frac{2}{75}}\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}.

By (
4.15
), Cramer’s rule and Lemma
3.6
, one has

\displaystyle\|(\mathcal{T}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}-\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}\mathcal{R}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}})^{-1}\|_{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|\det\mathcal{S}_{s+1}(\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega})|^{-1}\|(\mathcal{T}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}-\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}\mathcal{R}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}})^{*}\|_{0}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle(2^{s+1})^{2}(4|v|_{R})^{2^{s+1}}\delta_{s+1}^{-\frac{2}{75}}\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}.

Recalling (
4.101
), we get since Lemma
A.2

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\|_{0}

\displaystyle<4(1+\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\|_{0})^{2}

\displaystyle\ \ \times(1+\|(\mathcal{T}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}-\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}\mathcal{R}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\mathcal{R}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}\mathcal{T}\mathcal{R}_{A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}})^{-1}\|_{0})

\displaystyle<\delta_{s+1}^{-\frac{1}{15}}\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}.

(4.116)

For the case of

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\bm{l}_{s}}{2}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{0}-\frac{1}{2}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s}^{\frac{2}{3}},

(4.117)

we have

\displaystyle P_{s+1}\subset\{\bm{k}\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=0}^{s}\bm{l}_{i}:\ \left\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\frac{1}{2}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}<3\delta_{s}^{\frac{2}{3}}\}.

(4.118)

Now we consider
\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)
in the set

\displaystyle\{z\in\mathbb{C}:\ \left|z-\frac{1}{2}\right|<\delta_{s}^{\frac{5}{8}}\}.

(4.119)

The similar argument shows that
\det\mathcal{M}_{s+1}(z)=0
has two roots
\theta_{s+1}
and
1-\theta_{s+1}
in the set of (
4.119
) such that (
4.108
)–(
4.116
) hold true for
z
being in the set of (
4.119
). Hence if (
4.117
), then (
4.108
)–(
4.116
) hold true for
z
being in

\displaystyle\{z\in\mathbb{C}:\ \left\|z-\frac{1}{2}\right\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s}^{\frac{5}{8}}\}.

By (
4.118
), for
\bm{k}\in P_{s+1}
, we also have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\|_{0}<\delta_{s+1}^{-\frac{1}{15}}\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}-\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}\cdot\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}^{-1}.

Therefore, we have established desired estimates of
\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\|_{0}

for both cases of
(\bm{C}1)_{s}
and
(\bm{C}2)_{s}
.

Step2

:
Estimates of
\|\mathcal{T}_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\|_{\alpha}
for
\alpha\in(0,\alpha_{1}]
.

The main result of this step is Theorem
4.9
. Recalling

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\gamma}{\delta_{s+1}}\right)=\left(\frac{\gamma}{\delta_{s}}\right)^{30}

and

\displaystyle Q_{s+1}^{\pm}=\{\bm{k}\in P_{s+1}:\ \|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}\pm\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}<\delta_{s+1}\},\ Q_{s+1}=Q_{s+1}^{+}\cup Q_{s+1}^{-},

we have

Theorem 4.9
.

For
\bm{k}\in P_{s+1}\setminus Q_{s+1}
and
\alpha\in(0,\alpha_{1}]
, one has

\displaystyle\|T_{\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\|_{\alpha}<\zeta_{s+1}^{\alpha}\delta_{s+1}^{-\frac{7}{3}}.

Remark 4.3
.

Actually, for
\alpha\in(0,\alpha_{1}]
, we can prove

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}}^{-1}\|_{\alpha}\lesssim\zeta_{s+1}^{\alpha}\delta_{s+1}^{-\frac{1}{3}}A^{-2}

provided
\min\limits_{\sigma=\pm 1}\|\theta+\bm{k}\cdot\bm{\omega}+\sigma\theta_{s+1}\|_{\mathbb{T}}\geq A>0
and
\zeta_{s+1}^{-\alpha_{1}}\zeta_{s}^{\alpha_{1}}\delta_{s+1}^{-\frac{1}{3}}A^{-2}\ll 1
.

Similar to the proof of Theorem
4.4
, the proof of Theorem
4.9
also builds on several lemmas.

Let
G=\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\setminus\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
which is
s
-
{\rm good}
(cf. Subsection 3.3 of
[
CSZ24b
]
) and
B=\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}
. Thus
X:=\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}=B\cup G
,
G\cap A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}=\emptyset
and
A_{\bm{k}}^{s+1}\subset B
.

We have

Lemma 4.10
.

Let
\varepsilon_{0}=\varepsilon_{0}(\alpha_{1},\alpha_{0},d,\tau,\gamma,v,R,\phi)\ll 1
. Then there exist
\mathcal{M}\in\mathbf{M}_{X}^{G}
and
\mathcal{N}\in\mathbf{M}_{B}^{G}
satisfying

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}\mathcal{T}_{X}=\mathcal{R}_{G}+\mathcal{N}.

(4.120)

with the following estimates:

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{N}\|_{0}\lesssim 1,\ \|\mathcal{M}\|_{0}\lesssim\tilde{\zeta}_{s}^{\alpha_{0}}\delta_{s}^{-\frac{31}{15}},

and for
\alpha\in(0,\alpha_{1}]
,

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{N}\|_{\alpha}\lesssim\zeta_{s}^{\alpha}\tilde{\zeta}_{s}^{\alpha_{0}}\delta_{s}^{-\frac{7}{3}},\ \|\mathcal{M}\|_{\alpha}\lesssim\zeta_{s}^{\alpha}\tilde{\zeta}_{s}^{2\alpha_{0}}\delta_{s}^{-\frac{22}{5}}.

(4.121)

Proof.

Define

\displaystyle\tilde{P}_{t}=\{\bm{j}\in P_{t}:\ \exists\bm{j}^{\prime}\in Q_{t-1}\ \text{s.t.}\ \tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{j}^{\prime}}^{t-1}\subset X,\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{j}^{\prime}}^{t-1}\subset\Omega_{\bm{j}}^{t}\},\ 1\leq t\leq s+1.

(4.122)

From (
4.13
), (
4
) and (
4.122
), it follows that for
\bm{j}^{\prime}\in\tilde{P}_{t}\cap Q_{t}\ (1\leq t\leq s)
, there exists a
\bm{j}\in\tilde{P}_{t+1}
such that

\displaystyle\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{j}^{\prime}}^{t}\subset\Omega_{\bm{j}}^{t+1}

and

\displaystyle\tilde{P}_{s+1}\cap Q_{s+1}=\emptyset.

Moreover,

\displaystyle\bigcup_{1\leq t\leq s+1}\bigcup_{\bm{j}\in\tilde{P}_{t}}\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{j}}^{t}\subset X.

Hence for any
\bm{l}\in G
, if

\displaystyle\bm{l}\in\bigcup_{\bm{j}\in\tilde{P}_{1}}\Omega_{\bm{j}}^{1},

then there exists a
t\in[1,s]
such that

\displaystyle\bm{l}\in\bigcup_{\bm{j}\in\tilde{P}_{t}\setminus Q_{t}}\Omega_{\bm{j}}^{t}.

For every
\bm{l}\in G
, define

\displaystyle U_{\bm{l}}=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\Lambda_{\frac{1}{2}N_{1}}(\bm{l})\cap X,&\text{if}\ \bm{l}\notin\bigcup_{\bm{j}\in\tilde{P}_{1}}\Omega_{\bm{j}}^{1},\\
\tilde{\Omega}_{\bm{j}}^{t},&\text{if}\ \bm{l}\in\Omega_{\bm{j}}^{t}\ \text{for some}\ \bm{j}\in\tilde{P}_{t}\setminus Q_{t}.\end{array}\right.

Let
\mathcal{Q}_{\bm{l}}=\varepsilon\mathcal{T}_{U_{\bm{l}}}^{-1}\mathcal{W}_{X\setminus U_{\bm{l}}}^{U_{\bm{l}}}\in\mathbf{M}_{X\setminus U_{\bm{l}}}^{U_{\bm{l}}}
.
We now vary
\bm{l}\in G
and define

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}(\bm{l},\bm{l}^{\prime})=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}0,&\text{for}\ \bm{l}^{\prime}\in U_{\bm{l}}\\
\mathcal{Q}_{\bm{l}}(\bm{l},\bm{l}^{\prime}),&\text{for}\ \bm{l}^{\prime}\in X\setminus U_{\bm{l}},\end{array}\right.

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}(\bm{l},\bm{l}^{\prime})=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\mathcal{T}_{U_{\bm{l}}}^{-1}(\bm{l},\bm{l}^{\prime}),&\text{for}\ \bm{l}^{\prime}\in U_{\bm{l}}\\
0,&\text{for}\ \bm{l}^{\prime}\in X\setminus U_{\bm{l}}.\end{array}\right.

Then we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}\mathcal{T}_{X}=\mathcal{R}_{G}+\mathcal{K}.

(4.123)

Next, we estimate
\mathcal{K}\in\mathbf{M}_{X}^{G}
and
\mathcal{L}\in\mathbf{M}_{X}^{G}
. We have the following cases.

(1) Let
\bm{l}\notin\bigcup_{\bm{j}\in\tilde{P}_{1}}\Omega_{\bm{j}}^{1}
. Then
U_{\bm{l}}
is
0
-good. Similar to the proof of Lemma
4.4
, we have for
\alpha\in[0,\alpha_{1}],

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}^{\{\bm{l}\}}\|_{\alpha_{0}}\lesssim N_{1}^{-\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{0}},

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}^{\{\bm{l}\}}\|_{\alpha+\alpha_{0}}\lesssim\delta_{0}^{28}<1,

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{L}^{\{\bm{l}\}}\|_{\alpha+\alpha_{0}}\lesssim\delta_{0}^{-2}.

(2) Let
\bm{l}\in\left(\bigcup_{\bm{j}\in\tilde{P}_{1}}\Omega_{\bm{k}}^{1}\right)\bigcap G
. Then there exist some
t\in[1,s]
and
\bm{j}^{\prime}\in\tilde{P}_{t}\setminus Q_{t}
such that
\bm{l}\in\Omega_{\bm{j}^{\prime}}^{t}
. Then by (
3.1
) and (
4.18
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{Q}_{\bm{l}}\|_{\alpha_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathcal{T}_{U_{\bm{l}}}^{-1}\|_{\alpha_{1}}\|\mathcal{W}_{X}\|_{\alpha_{1}}\lesssim\zeta_{t}^{\alpha_{1}}\delta_{t}^{-\frac{7}{3}}.

(4.124)

By (
3.1
), (
3.4
) and (
4.18
), we have for
\alpha\in(0,\alpha_{1}]
,

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{Q}_{\bm{l}}\|_{\alpha+\alpha_{0}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathcal{T}_{U_{\bm{l}}}^{-1}\|_{\alpha+\alpha_{0}}\|\mathcal{W}_{X}\|_{0}+\|\mathcal{T}_{U_{\bm{l}}}^{-1}\|_{0}\|\mathcal{W}_{X}\|_{\alpha+\alpha_{0}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\tilde{\zeta}_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\|\mathcal{T}_{U_{\bm{l}}}^{-1}\|_{\alpha}\|\mathcal{W}_{X}\|_{\alpha+\alpha_{0}}\lesssim\zeta_{t}^{\alpha}\tilde{\zeta}_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\delta_{t}^{-\frac{7}{3}}.

Note that if
\bm{l}^{\prime}\in X\setminus U_{\bm{l}}
, then
\|\bm{l}-\bm{l}^{\prime}\|\geq\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{t}}{2}
. This implies
\mathcal{K}^{\{\bm{l}\}}(\bm{l},\bm{l}^{\prime})=0
for
\|\bm{l}-\bm{l}^{\prime}\|<\frac{\tilde{\zeta}_{t}}{2}
. By (
3.3
) and (
4.124
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}^{\{\bm{l}\}}\|_{\alpha_{0}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\tilde{\zeta}_{t}^{-\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{0}}\|\mathcal{K}^{\{\bm{l}\}}\|_{\alpha_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\tilde{\zeta}_{t}^{-\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{0}}\|\mathcal{Q}_{\bm{l}}\|_{\alpha_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\zeta_{t}^{\alpha_{1}}\tilde{\zeta}_{t}^{-\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{0}}\delta_{t}^{-\frac{7}{3}}.

Similarly, for
\alpha\in(0,\alpha_{1}],
we obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}^{\{\bm{l}\}}\|_{\alpha+\alpha_{0}}\leq\|\mathcal{Q}_{\bm{l}}\|_{\alpha+\alpha_{0}}\lesssim\zeta_{t}^{\alpha}\tilde{\zeta}_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\delta_{t}^{-\frac{7}{3}}.

By the definition of
U_{\bm{l}}
, if
\|\bm{l}-\bm{l}^{\prime}\|>2\tilde{\zeta}_{t}
, then
\bm{l}^{\prime}\notin U_{\bm{l}}
. This implies
\mathcal{L}^{\{\bm{l}\}}(\bm{l},\bm{l}^{\prime})=0
for
\|\bm{l}-\bm{l}^{\prime}\|>2\tilde{\zeta}_{t}
. By (
3.4
), (
4.17
) and (
4.18
), we have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{L}^{\{\bm{l}\}}\|_{\alpha_{0}}\lesssim\tilde{\zeta}_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\|\mathcal{L}^{\{\bm{l}\}}\|_{0}\leq\tilde{\zeta}_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\|T_{U_{\bm{l}}}^{-1}\|_{0}<\tilde{\zeta}_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\delta_{t}^{-\frac{31}{15}},

and for
\alpha\in(0,\alpha_{1}]
,

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{L}^{\{\bm{l}\}}\|_{\alpha+\alpha_{0}}\lesssim\tilde{\zeta}_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\|\mathcal{L}^{\{\bm{l}\}}\|_{\alpha}\leq\tilde{\zeta}_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\|T_{U_{\bm{l}}}^{-1}\|_{\alpha}<\zeta_{t}^{\alpha}\tilde{\zeta}_{t}^{\alpha_{0}}\delta_{t}^{-\frac{7}{3}}.

To sum up, by (
3.5
), we can get for
\alpha\in(0,\alpha_{1}],

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}\|_{0}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{\bm{l}\in G}\|\mathcal{K}^{\{\bm{l}\}}\|_{\alpha_{0}}\lesssim\max\{\zeta_{1}^{\alpha_{1}}\tilde{\zeta}_{1}^{-\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{0}}\delta_{1}^{-\frac{7}{3}},N_{1}^{-\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{0}}\}\leq\frac{1}{2},

(4.125)

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{K}\|_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{\bm{l}\in G}\|\mathcal{K}^{\{\bm{l}\}}\|_{\alpha+\alpha_{0}}\lesssim\zeta_{s}^{\alpha}\tilde{\zeta}_{s}^{\alpha_{0}}\delta_{s}^{-\frac{7}{3}},

(4.126)

and

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{L}\|_{0}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{\bm{l}\in G}\|\mathcal{L}^{\{\bm{l}\}}\|_{\alpha_{0}}\lesssim\tilde{\zeta}_{s}^{\alpha_{0}}\delta_{s}^{-\frac{31}{15}},

(4.127)

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{L}\|_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\bm{l}\in G}\|\mathcal{L}^{\{\bm{l}\}}\|_{\alpha+\alpha_{0}}\lesssim\zeta_{s}^{\alpha}\tilde{\zeta}_{s}^{\alpha_{0}}\delta_{s}^{-\frac{7}{3}}.

(4.128)

Finally, by recalling Lemma
3.5
, we have that
\mathcal{I}_{G}+\mathcal{K}_{G}
is invertible and

\displaystyle\|(\mathcal{I}_{G}+\mathcal{K}_{G})^{-1}\|_{\alpha}

\displaystyle\lesssim\min\{1,\|\mathcal{K}\|_{\alpha}\}\ \text{for}\ \alpha\in[0,\alpha_{1}].

(4.129)

From (
4.123
), we have

\displaystyle(\mathcal{I}_{G}+\mathcal{K}_{G})^{-1}\mathcal{L}\mathcal{T}_{X}=\mathcal{R}_{G}+(\mathcal{I}_{G}+\mathcal{K}_{G})^{-1}\mathcal{K}\mathcal{R}_{B}.

Let

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}=(\mathcal{I}_{G}+\mathcal{K}_{G})^{-1}\mathcal{L}\in\mathbf{M}_{X}^{G},\ \mathcal{N}=(\mathcal{I}_{G}+\mathcal{K}_{G})^{-1}\mathcal{K}\mathcal{R}_{B}\in\mathbf{M}_{B}^{G},

then

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}\mathcal{T}_{X}=\mathcal{R}_{G}+\mathcal{N}.

By recalling (
3.1
) and (
4.125
)–(
4.129
), we have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{N}\|_{0}

\displaystyle\leq\|(\mathcal{I}_{G}+\mathcal{K}_{G})^{-1}\|_{0}\|\mathcal{K}\|_{0}\lesssim 1,

(4.130)

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{M}\|_{0}
